Schema level: `Bottom.check(*checks)`, `S.Literal` / `S.Literals` (`literals`, `members`, `pick`, `map_members`), `S.Enum`, `S.Union(*members, mode=)` (`members`, `map_members(f, unsafe_preserve_checks=)`), `S.refine`, `S.from_brand`, `S.make_filter(_group)`, `S.resolve_annotations`, the refined primitives `Finite`, `Int`, `Natural`, `Port`, `NonEmptyString`, `Char`, `Trimmed`, and the checks of `_checks_string.py` (`is_pattern`, `is_trimmed`, `is_uuid(version)`, `is_guid`, `is_ulid`, `is_base64(_url)`, `is_string_finite`, `is_string_big_int`, `is_starting_with` / `ending_with` / `including`, the case checks), `_checks_number.py` (`is_finite`, `is_int`, `is_int32`, `is_uint32`, bounds, `is_between`, `is_multiple_of`, and their `_big_int` / `_date` variants), `_checks_size.py` (length, code points, size, properties, `is_property_names`, `is_unique`, `is_unique_key`) and `_checks_order.py` (`make_is_greater_than` ... `make_is_multiple_of` for any ordered type); each carries Effect's `representation` annotation. `pyfect.brand` now runs schema checks: `Brand.checks`, `check(*checks)`, `make(predicate)` = one `make_filter`, and `BrandError(issue)` whose `message` is the formatted issue (`brand.Filter` is gone). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_schema_checks.py` (the Literal, Literals, Union-of-primitives, Enum, Checks, Check, fromBrand, resolveAnnotations, Int, Natural, Trimmed, parse-option, `Schema.makeFilter`, `Union options` and `Union.mapMembers / appendElement` titles of `Schema.test.ts`; omissions listed), `test_schema_code_points.py` (`schema/CodePoints.test.ts`), `test_schema_issue.py` (every title now), `test_brand.py` on real checks; `tests/test_schema_checks.py` pins more behaviours on Python values, messages printed by Effect under Node. Pythonic decisions: `S.Int` is an `int` schema (`1.0` and `True` are not integers), `is_int` on a number also accepts integral floats; lengths count code points (`len`), not UTF-16 units; `Enum` takes an `enum.Enum` and decodes a value (or member) to its member, encoding to the value; union modes are `"any_of"` / `"one_of"`; Effect's `RangeError`s are `ValueError`s with its messages; `fromBrand` takes no name (a brand is a `NewType`). Not ported: `brand(identifier)` (a `NewType` is the brand), `isStringSymbol`, BigDecimal checks, `checkEffect`, `TemplateLiteral`, sentinels, and the titles needing `Struct`, `Record`, `Array`, `Void`, `suspend` or transformations.

**Structs, records and classes status (2026-10-27): done but for the titles needing Array, Tuple, transformations or mapped fields.** AST: `Objects(properties, index_signatures)` with `PropertySignature(name, type, optional=, default=)` and `IndexSignature(parameter, type)` (any `Mapping` in, a new `dict` out; excess keys follow `on_excess_property`; `errors="all"` collects every key), `record(key, value)`, `Suspend(thunk)` (lazy, memoized), `Class(shape, side)` (decode / encode / type / encoded sides; checks apply to instances) and `AST.key_annotations` (Effect's `annotateKey`); `_candidates.py` is Effect's candidate index, dispatching union members on sentinels (`_tag: Literal["A"]`) as well as kinds, and naming them `{ readonly "_tag": "A", ... }`. Schema level: `S.Struct(TypedDict, **overrides)` (`StructSchema`, fields derived from the annotations by `_derive.py`, recursive TypedDicts through `Suspend`), `S.Record(key, value)` (`RecordSchema`, Type `dict[K, V]`), `S.suspend`, `Bottom.annotate_key`, `S.is_schema` (a `TypeIs`, true for schema classes), and the classes `S.Class`, `S.TaggedClass`, `S.Error`, `S.TaggedError` (`dataclass_transform`, frozen kw-only fields, `S.field(schema, default=, default_factory=)`, `@S.class_check`, `identifier=`, `frozen=`, `tag=`; class methods `fields()`, `make`, `make_option`, `make_effect`, `annotate`, `check`; the constructor validates; errors subclass `errors.TaggedError`, compare by fields and keep `add_note`). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_schema_struct.py` (20: Struct, parse options, Record, annotateKey), `test_schema_class.py` (37: Class, TaggedClass, Error, TaggedError), 10 more in `test_schema_checks.py` (the struct union and sentinel titles, the Record checks) and the `Class` half of `isSchema`; `tests/test_schema_structs.py` (11) pins derivation errors, recursion, defaults, constructor validation, tag dispatch, frozen errors and `catch_tag`. Not ported, each listed in its oracle docstring: `StructWithRest`, `mapFields` / `pick` / `omit` / `partial` / `extend` on structs (a TypedDict is the struct, so they are TypedDict inheritance or a new TypedDict), `optional` / `optionalKey` / `mutableKey` wrappers (`NotRequired`, `ReadOnly`), `withConstructorDefault` (field defaults), symbol keys, `Undefined`-valued keys, and the titles needing Array, Tuple or transformations. Pythonic decisions: a defaulted key is optional when decoding and on the encoded side; `make` takes an instance (construction is the constructor); a class decodes from a mapping, not from an instance; an empty struct accepts any `Mapping`; `Enum` record keys are an index signature. Review: a suspended union member takes any input and is never made to select candidates (Effect's `toCandidate`; a union containing itself used to recurse forever), a defaulted literal field is no sentinel, decoded errors link a `cause` field to `__cause__`, and frozen dataclass errors (`errors.TaggedError` and the schema errors) survive `copy` / `pickle`; the "contradicted sentinels", "keeps suspended members lazy" and "does not force a recursive suspended member" union titles are ported. The suite has 4,873 tests.

**Tuples, arrays, collections and data-type schemas status (2026-10-27): done but for the transformation titles.** AST (`pyfect/schema/ast/`): `Arrays(elements, rest, mutable=)` with `Element(type, optional=)` (`_arrays.py`, Effect's `Arrays`: a `list` or `tuple` in, a `tuple` out, or a `list` when `mutable`; a required element after an optional one is Effect's `ValueError` "A required element cannot follow an optional element. ts(1257)"; post-rest elements, `MissingKey` with key annotations, excess indexes as `UnexpectedKey`, `errors="all"`), `Declaration(type_parameters, run)` (`_declaration.py`; the parser is made on first use and memoized, a raising parser is a defect, flipping flips the parameters) with `parse_at_key` (Effect's `makeCompositeAtKey`), and `is_json` / `json` (`_json.py`, iterative, cycles rejected, DAGs accepted). The union candidate index (`_candidates.py`) now dispatches tuples on their required literal elements and names a tuple member `readonly [ "a", ... ]`. Schemas: `Tuple(*elements)` (exact overloads up to 8, `.elements`, `map_elements`), `optional_key`, `TupleWithRest(schema, rest, *post)`, `Array` (a `tuple[A, ...]`), `List` (a `list`, Effect's `mutable(Array)`), `NonEmptyArray`, `UniqueArray`, `FrozenSet`, `Map`, `Option`, `Result`, `CauseReason`, `Cause`, `Exit`, `Redacted(value, label=)`, `Duration`, `DateTimeUtc`, `DateTimeZoned`, `TimeZoneNamed`, `TimeZoneOffset`, `TimeZone`, `ByteSize`, `Json` / `JsonObject` / `JsonValue`, `declare(guard)`, `instance_of(cls)` and `declare_constructor(type_parameters, run)` (`_arrays.py`, `_collections.py`, `_data.py`, `_values.py`, `_declare.py`). Derivation now covers `list[X]`, `tuple[X, ...]`, fixed tuples, `Sequence[X]`, `frozenset[X]` / `AbstractSet[X]`, `Redacted[X]` and `timedelta`; a variadic `tuple[X, *tuple[Y, ...]]` has no derived schema (`S.field(S.TupleWithRest(...))`). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_schema_composites.py` (54 titles of `Schema.test.ts`: Tuple, Array, NonEmptyArray, TupleWithRest, suspend, Redacted, Option, Result, Cause / CauseReason / Exit, the collection, date-time and zone titles, instanceOf, Tuple.mapElements, Union.mapMembers, UniqueArray, refine, the tuple sentinel unions and annotateKey on a tuple; `FiniteFromString` stands as `Finite` until transformations), `tests/oracle/test_schema_ast.py` (15 titles of `SchemaAST.test.ts`: isJson, Suspend, getCandidateIndex), and the Class-in-Array title in `test_schema_class.py`; omitted titles are listed with reasons in each docstring. `tests/test_schema_composites.py` pins 24 behaviours printed by Effect under Node first (`report_input` messages, missing positions under `errors="all"`, checks kept by `TupleWithRest`, a list against a union of tuple, struct and array members, redacted values never shown, declaration defects). Review: every schema class now declares covariant type parameters (pyright had inferred them invariant, so `TupleWithRest(Tuple(String), rest_needing_a_service)` did not type-check; `tests/test_schema_variance.py` and `check_schema_classes_are_covariant` guard it), the sentinel candidate index no longer slows tagged-struct unions (an extra lookup call and kind scan per input had cost about 25%), and `optional_key` idempotency and the once-made declaration parser are tested. The suite has 4,970 tests.

**Getters, transformations and codecs status (2026-10-27): done but for the `undefined`-based APIs and a few codecs.** Parsing now returns a `ParseResult` (`pyfect/schema/ast/_result.py`): an `Exit` when every step ran synchronously, else the `Effect` that finishes it, so a schema without effectful getters stays as fast as before (`AST.parse` inlines `parse_local`; a composite is a `Steps` in `ast/_steps.py` that runs synchronously until a child's parse is an effect and then resumes in one). `MISSING` stands for an absent key or element; getters see it as `None` (Effect's `Option.none()`). `pyfect.schema.getter` (`G`): `Getter[T, E, R]` with `pipe`, `compose` and `map`, the constructors `transform`, `transform_optional`, `transform_effect`, `transform_optional_effect`, `passthrough` (and `_supertype` / `_subtype`, typed `Any` because Python cannot state the subtyping), `succeed`, `fail`, `forbidden`, `forbidden_encoding`, `required`, `omit`, `with_default`, `check_effect`, the coercions (`string`, `number`, `boolean`, `big_int`, `date_time_utc_from_input`, with JavaScript's conversion rules), the text getters (`trim`, `capitalize`, `snake_to_camel`, `split_key_value`, `encode_uri_component`, ...), JSON (`parse_json`, `stringify_json(space=)`) and Base64 / Base64Url / Hex. `pyfect.schema.transformation` (`T`): `Transformation(decode, encode)` with `flip`, `compose`, and `Middleware`, plus Effect's built-in transformations (`trim`, `number_from_string`, `big_int_from_string`, `url_from_string`, `date_time_utc_from_string`, the time-zone, byte-size, Base64 / Hex / URI ones, `from_json_string`, `option_from_null_or`, `option_from_optional_key`). Schema level (`pyfect/schema/_transform.py`, `_middleware.py`, `_codecs.py`): curried `decode_to` / `encode_to` (with or without a transformation), `decode`, `encode`, `flip` (`FlipSchema.schema`; flipping twice gives the original), `to_type`, `to_encoded`, `middleware_decoding` / `middleware_encoding` (the result needs the services the middleware leaves, Effect's typing), `catch_decoding` / `catch_encoding` (which also cover `catch*WithContext`: a handler may need services), `with_decoding_default_key` / `with_decoding_default_type_key(encoding_strategy=)` (a default that is already an `Exit` keeps decoding synchronous), and the codecs `NumberFromString`, `FiniteFromString`, `BigIntFromString`, `Trim`, `StringFromBase64(Url)` / `Hex` / `UriComponent`, `BytesFromBase64(Url)` / `Hex`, `URLFromString`, `BooleanFromBit`, `DateTimeUtcFromString` / `FromMillis`, `DateTimeZonedFromString`, `TimeZoneNamedFromString`, `TimeZoneFromString`, `ByteSizeFromString` / `BigInt` / `Number`, `from_json_string(schema, space=)`, `UnknownFromJsonString`, `NullOr`, `OptionFromNullOr`, `OptionFromOptionalKey` and `RedactedFromValue`, with the schemas `URL` (a `urllib.parse.SplitResult`) and `Bytes` (`bytes`, Effect's `Uint8Array`). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_schema_getter.py` (17, `SchemaGetter.test.ts` and `SchemaTransformation.test.ts`), `test_schema_transform.py` (41: the Transformations, decodeTo, encodeTo, flip, codec and Option titles) and `test_schema_middleware.py` (31: catch / middleware, optional fields, `asOption`, `checkEffect`, the decoding defaults); `testing.SchemaAsserts` is generic in the decoding and encoding services, and its assertions only type-check once `provide(tag, service)` gave a direction its service. Fixed on the way, each with a failing test first: a struct's `NotRequired` keys written under `from __future__ import annotations` were required (Python's `__optional_keys__` misses string annotations; `_derive.optional_keys` reads the evaluated hints, `tests/test_schema_typed_dict_keys.py`); a child failure mixing an issue with a defect lost its path (`ast.at_key`, as Effect's `Composite` of a `Pointer` over the cause, `tests/test_schema_mixed_cause.py`). `tests/test_typing_rules.py` now flags two `Never`-defaulted TypeVars only when they meet in one union (the hazard it guards), so a schema's separate `RD` / `RE` slots may both default. Pythonic decisions: `with_default` replaces a missing value only (Python has no `undefined`); `stringify_json` rejects `NaN` and infinities; the JSON reviver / replacer options are not ported. Not ported: `optional`, `UndefinedOr`, `OptionFromUndefinedOr`, `OptionFromNullishOr`, `withDecodingDefault` / `withDecodingDefaultType` (all `undefined`); the `Duration` codecs (`timedelta` has no infinity and only microseconds); the `Date` and `BigDecimal` codecs; `makeTreeRecord`, `FormData` and `URLSearchParams`; v3's `transformOrFail`. Performance (`decode` of 100 structs, 1 union member, run against the commit before the cluster): 298 µs vs 270 µs and 1.71 µs vs 1.60 µs; the cost is the `MISSING` and `ParseResult` checks on the hot path.

**Getters, transformations and codecs review (2026-10-27).** A second pass, each fix with a failing test first. The `concurrency` parse option was declared but ignored: a product's effectful children now run up to the limit (`ast/_steps.py` `_resume_concurrently`, Effect's `iterateConcurrent`): workers take the next step from a shared cursor, record outcomes in completion order, and the step that ends the parse (`errors="first"` or a defect) interrupts the others; positions are kept whatever order elements finish in, unions stay sequential, and a limit below 1 is a `ValueError` at the adapter (`tests/test_schema_concurrency.py`). A getter, transformation or middleware raising synchronously escaped as a Python exception; `ast.call` now turns it into a defect, as Effect's `Exit.die`. `Base64Url` decoding now follows Effect (CR / LF dropped, a length of 1 mod 4 rejected, then padded; `tests/test_schema_codecs.py`, every value printed by Effect under Node). `ByteSizeFromNumber` accepted an `int` above `2**53 - 1`; a Python `int` read as a number must now be a safe integer. Added `OptionFromOptionalNullOr` (`on_none_encoding="omit"` or `None`). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_schema_parser.py` gains the `SchemaParser.test.ts` titles on callback defects, sequential parsing, product concurrency and effectful `decodeUnknownExit` (its docstring lists the omissions); `tests/oracle/test_schema_codecs.py` ports the codec titles of `Schema.test.ts` (Base64 / Base64Url / Hex / URI, `DateTimeUtcFromString` / `Millis`, `ByteSizeFrom*`, `URLFromString`, `Trim`, `transformEffect`, `toLowerCase` / `toUpperCase`); the container titles of `test_schema_composites.py` now use `FiniteFromString` as Effect does. Known differences left: `DateTimeUtcFromString` accepts `"20240101"`, which `Date.parse` rejects, and rejects V8's legacy forms (`"1"`); `TimeZoneFromString` keeps `"GMT"` and does not canonicalise a zone key's case. Not ported yet: `DateTimeUtcFromHttpDate`, `DateTimeUtcFromDate`, the network-address codecs. Suite: 5,139 tests.

**JSON codec, JSON Schema and JSON tools status (2026-10-27): done but for the schemas not ported yet.** `pyfect.json_pointer` (`parse_uri_fragment`, `format_uri_fragment`, `escape_token`, `unescape_token`), `pyfect.json_patch` (`get`, `apply`, typed `JsonPatchOperation`s; a bad operation is a type error) and `pyfect.json_schema` (`Document` / `MultiDocument` typed by dialect, `from_schema_draft_07` / `_2020_12` / `from_schema_open_api_3_0` / `_3_1`, `to_document_draft_07` / `_04`, `to_multi_document_open_api_3_1`, `rewrite_refs`; a Draft-07 document passed where Draft-04 is expected is a type error) port `JsonPointer.ts`, `JsonPatch.ts` and `JsonSchema.ts`. `S.to_codec_json(schema)` (Effect's `toCodecJson`, `pyfect/schema/_to_codec_json.py` with the links in `_codec_links.py`, `_data_codecs.py`, `_value_codecs.py`) gives a `Bottom[T, JsonValue, RD, RE]`: non-finite numbers as `"Infinity"` / `"-Infinity"` / `"NaN"`, bigints as digits, `Unknown` checked as JSON, declarations through their `to_codec_json` / `to_codec` annotation (`Option`, `Result`, `Exit`, `Cause`, `Redacted`, `Duration` as `Nanos` / `Millis`, `ByteSize`, date-times, URLs, `bytes` as Base64, `FrozenSet` as an array, `Map` as pairs), `int` record keys read from their text; it returns the input node when nothing changes and is idempotent (a derived `Suspend` is marked by its thunk). `S.to_json_schema_document` / `S.to_json_schema_multi_document` (Effect's `toJsonSchemaDocument`, `pyfect/schema/_jsonschema/`: `_references.py` allocates `$defs` with Effect's naming and suffixes, `_compile.py` / `_objects.py` compile nodes, `_checks.py` maps each built-in check by its `representation` id, `_merge.py` inlines check fragments) describe the encoded side of that codec, with `on_excess_property`, `generate_descriptions`, `include_annotation_key` and a `ReferencePolicy`; a `oneOf` with an approximate branch is loosened to `anyOf`; a user check gives its own fragment with a `to_json_schema` annotation (`CheckContext` in, `CheckOutput` out). Python differences: string length bounds are exact (`len` counts code points, as `minLength` does), `Int` is `{"type": "integer"}` without safe bounds, an `int` record key is selected by an exact pattern, `Duration` has no infinities, and `is_pattern` (a Python `re`) is left out. Ports: `tests/oracle/test_json_pointer.py` (52), `test_json_patch.py` (82), `test_json_schema.py` (142), `test_to_json_schema_document.py` (147) and `test_to_codec_json.py` (54), each omitted title listed with its reason (symbols, `undefined`, JS regexp flags and surrogates, Ajv validation, and the schemas not ported yet: `TemplateLiteral`, `StructWithRest`, `ErrorInstance` / `Defect`, `RegExp`, `from_json_string`'s `contentSchema`). Two bugs found by the ports, each with a failing test first: `to_codec_json` was not idempotent through `Suspend`, and a class's type side (`S.to_type(A)`) was encoded as opaque JSON instead of by its fields (the `Class` sides are now matched exhaustively). `assert_type` pins cover the codec's types, both documents and the option literals. `S.to_json_schema_multi_document((a, b, ...))` takes a non-empty tuple and stands for Effect's `SchemaRepresentation.toJsonSchemaMultiDocument(toRepresentations(...))` over `toCodecJson`, since `SchemaRepresentation` is not ported. Not ported: `SchemaRepresentation` (documents, revivers, code generation, `fromJsonSchemaDocument`), `toCodecIso`, `toCodecStringTree`, `toCodecGoJson`.

**Equivalence and formatter status (2026-10-27): done but for the schemas not ported yet.** `S.to_equivalence(schema)` (Effect's `toEquivalence`, `pyfect/schema/_to_equivalence.py`) is a `Callable[[T, T], bool]`: structs, tuples, arrays and records part by part (an index signature by the keys it selects), a union by the member both values belong to, a class by its fields; leaves use Effect's `Equal.equals` on Python values (`==`, but `True` is not `1` and `NaN` is `NaN`). `S.to_formatter(schema, on_before=)` (`_to_formatter.py`) is a `Callable[[T], str]` written as Effect does (`{ "a": some(1) }`, `[1, "a"]`, `A({ ... })`), anything else by `format_unknown` (Effect's `Formatter.format`). The built-in declarations carry `to_equivalence` / `to_formatter` annotations (`_instances.py`: `Option`, `Result`, `CauseReason`, `Cause`, `Exit`, `FrozenSet` / `Map` compared as unordered items and written sorted, `Redacted` by its value, `URL`, `DateTimeUtc` as `DateTime.Utc(...)`, zones as `+03:00` / their key, `DateTimeZoned` in RFC 9557); a checked declaration keeps them. `schema.override_to_equivalence(f)` / `override_to_formatter(f)` replace a schema's (typed by its values). `pyfect.date_time` gains `format_iso` and `zone_to_string` (Effect's `formatIso` / `zoneToString`). Python differences: a `BigInt` is written without `n`, a `timedelta` and a `bytes` value by their own text, an enum member as its value, `FrozenSet` / `Map` under those names (Effect's `ReadonlySet` / `ReadonlyMap`). Ports: `tests/oracle/test_to_equivalence.py` (41) and `test_to_formatter.py` (58), omissions listed (symbols, `undefined`, and the schemas not ported yet: `Date`, `RegExp`, `HashMap` / `HashSet` / `Chunk`, `BigDecimal`, `TemplateLiteral`, `StructWithRest`); `tests/test_schema_derivations.py` pins the union guard, `Equal.equals` on Python values, a property also selected by an index signature, and a checked declaration.

**JSON codec and JSON Schema review (2026-10-27).** Each fix with a failing test first, expected values printed by Effect under Node. A schema given twice to `to_json_schema_multi_document` (or a recursive one) became two definitions, because each root was derived for JSON on its own; the roots now share one derivation (`to_codec_json_asts`), as Effect's memoized `toCodecJson` gives (`tests/test_json_schema_references.py`, which also ports the reference-policy titles of `toRepresentations.test.ts` through the JSON Schema they produce, and pins unnamed recursive definitions: removing the recursion mark overflowed the stack and no test caught it). `JsonPatch.get` visited object keys and `toMultiDocumentOpenApi3_1` suffixed colliding component keys in Python's code-point order; JavaScript sorts by UTF-16 code units, so a key above U+FFFF came out in another order. Both use `_js.js_sort_key`, and `tests/test_js_string_order.py` fails on any `sorted(...)` without a `key` in the JSON ports. Ported titles the build had missed: "Class / Error preserves its identifier as a canonical reference" and "encoding a Redacted with a label" (`S.Redacted` takes a `label`); the `toCodecGoJson` titles are listed as not ported. Benchmarks: decoding 100 structs and 100 `Option(Duration)` values runs as fast as before the cluster (471 µs and 96 µs both).

**Hypothesis strategies and Optic status (2026-10-27): done.** `S.to_strategy(schema) -> SearchStrategy[T]` (Effect's `Arbitrary.schema`; Hypothesis runs, shrinks and replays, so none of Effect's runner is ported) needs the optional extra `pyfect[hypothesis]` (in the dev group; imported lazily, a `ModuleNotFoundError` naming the extra otherwise). The walk (`pyfect/schema/_arbitrary_graph.py`) types the schema once, finds the suspends on cycles (Tarjan, no recursion) and the least recursion each node needs (`INFINITE` for no finite value: `Never`, a struct requiring one, a loop with no exit), and rejects contradictory constraints before anything else (`Unable to derive an arbitrary for array constraints` / `object property constraints` / `constraints`) and schemas without a finite value (`... without a finite generation path`). Each node's checks give an `S.ArbitraryConstraint` (`_arbitrary_constraint.py`; built-in checks read from their `representation`, a custom check from an `arbitrary_constraint` annotation) that shapes the leaf and collection strategies (`_strategy/`), and every check still filters the draws. Strings with patterns draw from any one pattern's `from_regex`. Unions skip members over the recursion budget; a `one_of` union keeps only values one member takes; unique arrays use `unique_by` on Effect's structural equality. A declaration is drawn through its `to_arbitrary` annotation (a function of its type parameters' strategies, pyfect-only), else its `to_codec_arbitrary` link (given the type parameters and the constraint; the built-ins in `_arbitrary_links.py`: date-times at millisecond precision in years 1–9999, the five Effect zone names, whole-minute offsets, URLs, bytes, sets and maps honouring size bounds), else its `to_codec_json` / `to_codec`; `Json`, `Unknown` and `Any` draw JSON. `pyfect.optic` (`fx.optic`, Effect's `Optic`): `Iso`, `Lens`, `Prism`, `Optional` (with `Traversal` an `Optional` focusing a sequence) over plain data (`dict` / any `Mapping`, `list`, `tuple`, and values `copy.replace` updates: dataclasses, `S.Class`, named tuples; anything else is a `TypeError`), `Id[T]()` for `id<T>()`, `make_iso` / `make_lens` / `make_prism` / `make_optional`, the methods `compose` (the kind table of Effect), `key`, `optional_key`, `at`, `check` / `refine` / `tag` / `not_none` (`notUndefined`), `pick`, `omit`, `for_each`, `modify_all`, `get_all`, and `entries`, `some`, `none`, `success`, `failure`, `from_checks`; neighbouring keys and checks merge into one step, as in Effect. A missing key reads as `None` (`undefined`), a key's focus type is `Any` (Python cannot index a TypedDict by a type parameter), and the operations are methods, since none removes anything from a type. Ports: `tests/oracle/test_optic.py` (47 of 51 `Optic.test.ts` titles; 2 pyfect-only), `tests/oracle/test_arbitrary.py` (44 schema-derivation titles of `Arbitrary.test.ts`; the runner, shrinker, distribution and unported-schema titles listed as omitted), `tests/test_schema_round_trip.py` (every exported schema constant but `Never` and `ByteSizeFromNumber`, plus structs, a recursive struct, unions, options, maps and tuples: each drawn value is accepted and decodes back to an equivalent value through the schema and its JSON codec; `@given` composes with `@fx.testing.effect`). Bugs found by these properties, each with a failing test first: `DateTimeUtcFromMillis` lost a microsecond through float division (now exact integer arithmetic in `pyfect/date_time/_epoch.py`, used by every millisecond conversion), and a recursive schema's decoded side was an infinite graph, each use of a suspend giving a new node (`Suspend.to_type` now returns one node, which refers back to itself; `tests/test_schema_structs.py`). Not ported: `Schema.toIso` and the `Optic` re-exports of it, the `__proto__` title, string tuple indexes. `assert_type` pins cover `to_strategy` and the optic kinds.

**Hypothesis strategies and Optic review (2026-10-27).** Each fix with a failing test first. A `Number` bounded on both sides (`S.Number.check(S.is_between(minimum=0, maximum=10))`) could not be drawn at all: the float strategy asked Hypothesis for infinities between two finite bounds. The catalog had only bounded `Int` and `Finite`, and the titles that would have caught it ("preserves both signed zeros in an inclusive zero interval", "generates the sole Number between adjacent exclusive IEEE-754 bounds") were omitted as JavaScript edges; they are ported now, with "generates exact subnormal and infinite singleton intervals", "generates infinity from one-sided inclusive bounds", "rejects NaN numeric bounds", "rejects infinite intervals excluded by a finite constraint" and "derives mutually recursive declarations through toCodecArbitrary". Number, integer and bigint intervals are read once, in `_arbitrary_number.py`, and an empty one raises Effect's `Unable to derive an arbitrary for number / integer / bigint constraints` (an empty enum: `... an enum with no members`) when the strategy is derived, not when it is drawn. `is_property_names` crashed `to_strategy` (the constraint reader called every check's JSON Schema exporter as if it were a string check), and string checks' JavaScript patterns were read with Python's `$` and Unicode `\d`, so `is_string_big_int` drew mostly rejected strings; patterns now come only from the string checks, compiled as JavaScript (`js_regex`). Guard: `tests/test_schema_round_trip.py` round-trips one schema per exported check and fails when a new `is_*` check has no case. Two pyfect-only tests pin the recursion budget (values stay within it; a schema whose finite values need more recursion than `RECURSION_DEPTH` still draws, which fails if a cyclic suspend stops costing a level). Benchmarks (`benchmarks/runtime.py`) are unchanged.

**ChannelSchema, schema framers, Config.schema and adapters status (2026-10-27): done.** `pyfect.channel_schema` (`fx.channel_schema`, Effect's `ChannelSchema`): `decode` / `decode_unknown` / `encode` / `encode_unknown` (each chunk through `S.NonEmptyArray(schema)`, so a failure's path starts at the value's index in the chunk) and `duplex` / `duplex_unknown(channel, input_schema=, output_schema=)`. The framers gain their schema variants: `ndjson.encode_schema(_string)`, `decode_schema(_string)`, `duplex_schema(_string)`; `json_array.decode_schema(_string)`, `encode_schema(_string)`; `sse.decode_schema`, `decode_data_schema` (the `data` field read as JSON text), `encode_schema`, with `sse.EventSchema`, `EventEncoded` and `transform_event`. `encoding/_json.py` moved to `pyfect/_json_text.py` (the schema getters and the framers both need it, which made an import cycle). `S.to_codec_string_tree(schema)` (Effect's `toCodecStringTree`, `StringTree` type) writes every leaf as text (`"null"`, `"true"`, digits, `"Infinity"`, a non-string literal or enum value's text; a declaration through its `to_codec_string_tree`, else `to_codec_json` / `to_codec` link); it shares its walk with `to_codec_json` in `schema/_codec_derivation.py` (Effect's `makeCodecDerivation`). `config.schema(codec, path=)` (`pyfect/config/_schema.py`, Effect's `Config.schema`) derives the string-tree codec and adds one link per encoded node from a cursor (provider, path, node): objects load their declared keys and the record keys an index signature selects, arrays their items, scalars their own string; unions load each member by its own shape; a provider's `SourceError` travels as a defect so no union member recovers from it, then fails the config; missing input the codec rejects is absent (`with_default` applies); an opaque encoding (`Unknown`, `Any`, `Json`, a class's instance side) is a `ValueError` when the config is made. `list_of` / `dict_of` also take an element schema. Ports: `tests/oracle/test_config_schema.py` (74 titles: the schema container titles, "materialization", "fromEnv provider", "fromUnknown provider"; 7 omitted for `undefined` or schemas pyfect lacks, listed in its docstring), the `Config.schema` halves of the five partly ported titles in `test_config.py` (so all 175 `Config.test.ts` titles are ported or omitted with a reason), and the 7 schema titles of `Ndjson.test.ts`, `JsonArray.test.ts` and `Sse.test.ts`; `tests/test_channel_schema.py` and `tests/test_schema_string_tree.py` pin the rest (messages printed by Effect under Node). Adapters, each an optional extra in the dev group, imported only by its own module (`tests/test_optional_adapters.py` checks the sources and that `import pyfect` loads neither): `pyfect.pydantic` (`pyfect[pydantic]`): every schema object and schema class has `__get_pydantic_core_schema__` / `__get_pydantic_json_schema__`, so `Annotated[float, S.FiniteFromString]` or an `S.Class` is a pydantic field type (Python input decoded with the schema, a value its decoded side accepts kept, JSON input and `model_dump_json` through `S.to_codec_json`, the JSON Schema the schema's with definitions inlined, a failure one `pyfect_schema` error carrying the pyfect message; only schemas needing no services, enforced by the hook's `self` type), and `from_model(Model)` is a pyfect schema of a pydantic model over JSON values (each pydantic error an issue at its location); `pyfect.msgspec` (`pyfect[msgspec]`): `from_struct(Struct, strict=)` over `msgspec.convert` / `to_builtins`, the error at the path msgspec reports. `tests/test_pydantic.py`, `tests/test_msgspec.py` skip without the extras. `examples/schema.py` (run and pinned by `tests/test_examples.py`) decodes a JSON order with checks, fails with `SchemaError` or a domain `UnknownProduct`, maps each to a response with `catch_tag` and encodes the receipt back to JSON. The suite has 6,080 tests (one skipped: the extra-missing check).

**ChannelSchema, schema framers, Config.schema and adapters review (2026-10-27).** Each fix with a failing test first. A schema class that refers to itself (`friend: User | None`, a cycle through the lazy class shape rather than a `Suspend`) sent `to_codec_json`, `to_codec_string_tree`, `config.schema` and the pydantic hook into endless recursion: the derived class shapes (`_DerivedShape`, config's `_CursorShape`) derived their fields before the class node was memoized. They now derive them on first use and are started right after memoization, so errors still surface when the codec or config is made, and every type side of a class shares one derived shape (`tests/test_schema_recursive_class_codecs.py`, a pydantic case in `tests/test_pydantic.py`). Record keys lost the provider's order (`Record.keys` was a plain `frozenset`, so `config.schema` and `dict_of` sorted them): decoded records came out sorted and the first invalid key reported differed from Effect's (`{c: "y", a: "x"}` reports `c` under Node). `config_provider.Keys` keeps insertion order with set equality, and `from_unknown`, `from_env` and `from_dir` build it (`tests/test_config_key_order.py`).

**M8 completeness pass (2026-10-27): done; M8 is complete.** Effect test titles accounted for (ported, or omitted with a one-line reason, in `tests/oracle/`): 1449 of 2022 across `Schema.test.ts`, the `schema/` directory outside `representation/`, `JsonSchema.test.ts`, `JsonPatch.test.ts`, `JsonPointer.test.ts` and `Optic.test.ts` (1390 before the pass); `Schema.test.ts` 498/622, `SchemaParser.test.ts` 64/66, `SchemaAST.test.ts` 38/79, `toCodec.test.ts` 172/230, `toJsonSchemaDocument.test.ts` 186/225, `JsonSchema.test.ts` 119/121, `JsonPatch` / `JsonPointer` / `Optic` complete. Two bugs fixed, each with a failing test first: a union's candidate index ignored a recovering middleware (`catch_decoding`), so a member that takes any input was skipped by type dispatch (now `Middleware.sees_failures` and `_candidates._settled`, Effect's `toCandidate`); and a union member's expected message used its decoded side instead of its encoded side (now `to_encoded`). New: `S.ArrayEnsure`, `Bottom.annotate_encoded`, `S.resolve_annotations_key`, and ports of the parser laziness / retry / defect titles, the decoding adapters' defect and mixed-cause titles, and the struct and record transformation titles. Suite: 6171 passed, 1 skipped. Benchmarks match M7 (`benchmarks/runtime.py`: map chain 3.15M steps/s, gen 2.23M, fork+join 164k/s; `benchmarks/channel.py`: 0.93M items/s one by one, 20.35M chunked). Deferred, with reasons in the test docstrings: `TemplateLiteral` and `StructWithRest` (a typed template parser is its own design), `TaggedUnion` / `TaggedStruct` / `Opaque`, `BigDecimal`, `RegExp`, `ErrorInstance` / `Defect` JSON forms (§5 question 11), `HashMap` / `HashSet` / `Chunk` schemas (no such pyfect types), network / `SemVer` schemas, `SchemaRepresentation` (the `representation/` directory), `toIso` / `toCodecIso`, `toDifferJsonPatch`, `toEncoderXml`, `toCodecGoJson`, `toCodecArrayFromSingle`, `toStandardSchemaV1` / `toStandardJSONSchemaV1` (JavaScript's Standard Schema spec), `VariantSchema`, `Graph`, Promise adapters, and the AOT / JIT compilers (Effect-internal).

**M8 leftovers (2026-10-27): done.** The deferrals that needed only a design. `ErrorInstance` / `Defect` (§5 question 11, decided): an error is written as Effect writes one (`{name, message, stack?, cause?}`: the class name, `str(e)`, the formatted traceback under `include_stack=True`, the `__cause__` unless `exclude_cause=True`) and read back as `S.ErrorInstance`, a frozen dataclass of those fields, never as a class the input names. `S.ErrorInstance` is also the error schema (Type `BaseException | ErrorInstance`, `Expected Error`; `S.ErrorInstance.schema(include_stack=, exclude_cause=)`), and `S.Defect(...)` is `Json` decoded to an exception: a `Die` holds one, so a decoded defect is `S.DecodedDefect(value)` holding the `ErrorInstance` or the JSON value as it was. Both memoize per options. A `Cause` / `Exit` defect schema is now any `Codec[BaseException, object]` (the encoded side may be JSON). `S.TaggedStruct(TypedDict)` (a `_tag: Literal[...]` key, `.tag`), `S.tag_default_omit(literal)`, `S.to_tagged_union(tag)` / `S.TaggedUnion(*cases)` (`TaggedUnionSchema`: `tag`, `discriminants`, `cases`, `guards`, `is_any_of`; cases are TypedDict structs or `TaggedClass`es, nested unions flattened, duplicates rejected as in Effect); `match` / `matchOrElse` are Python's `match` statement, checked for exhaustiveness by pyright (pinned in `tests/type_checks.py`; `research/typing/schema_4_tagged.py`). `S.StructWithRest(struct, *records)` (Effect's checks, duplicate and encoding errors), and `S.Struct` reads PEP 728's `extra_items=` (`typing_extensions.TypedDict`) as an index signature over the undeclared keys (`research/typing/schema_5_extra_items.py`). `Opaque` is `S.Class` (a TypedDict cannot be nominal). One bug fixed with a failing test first: a derived Hypothesis strategy drew a declared key, or an index entry, that another index signature also takes without checking that signature's value type, so `to_strategy(StructWithRest(...))` drew invalid values; the objects plan now checks every entry against all the signatures taking its key (`_strategy`'s `fits_indexes`), and draws a declared key within the scalar checks of the signatures taking it (`_within`, Effect's combined constraints; an empty intersection gives up when drawing, as Effect's `SampleError`). Tests: `tests/oracle/test_schema_errors.py` (12), `test_schema_tagged.py` (12), `test_schema_struct_with_rest.py` (15), `tests/test_schema_extra_items.py` (5); the Arbitrary catalog draws `S.ErrorInstance` and `S.Defect()`. Not done: constructor defaults for a tag (`Schema.tag`'s `make` without `_tag`: a TypedDict value is built with its tag), and the JSON Schema of an `extra_items=` struct is permissive (`additionalProperties: true`) rather than typed.

### M9 — STM (L; depends on M2)
- **Order:** `tx`, `tx_retry`, Transaction service → TxRef → TxChunk / TxHashMap / TxHashSet (tuple, `HashMap`, `HashSet`; dict / frozenset until the core-gaps pass) → TxQueue / TxDeferred / TxSemaphore / TxReentrantLock / TxPriorityQueue → TxPubSub → TxSubscriptionRef (`changes_stream` after M6).
- **Exit criteria:** the `Effect.test.ts` transaction block and every `Tx*.test.ts`.

**Transactions core and TxRef (2026-10-27): done.** `fx.tx(eff)` (`pyfect.transaction`), `fx.tx_retry`, `fx.Transaction` (a tag whose service is `TransactionMeta`: `journal`, `attempt`, `retry_reason`, `start`, `now`, `elapsed`, `elapsed_since_previous`), `TransactionRetryReason`, `JournalEntry`, and `fx.TxRef` (`pyfect.tx_ref`: `TxRef(value)`, `make`, `get`, `set`, `update`, `modify`, `version`), following Effect v4's algorithm: the outermost `tx` keeps a journal of the version and value each ref had when first touched, reruns the body when a read ref's version moved (`"conflict"`) or after `tx_retry` (`"retry"`), and commits in the same fiber step as the check. `tx_retry` interrupts the body; once its finalizers ran, the fiber parks on the refs it read (checked again as it registers, so a commit landing in between reruns it at once) and a commit to any of them wakes it through the waiter's scheduler rather than in the committing step (Effect's `scheduleTask`), so an interrupt sent in that step still finds it parked, and a commit from another run (another thread's `run_sync`) still reaches it. A retry whose cleanup fails ends the transaction with that failure. Nested `tx` joins the running transaction; a child fiber inherits it until the boundary ends, then starts its own. A commit bumps a ref's version only when the value changed by `Object.is` (`pyfect._js.same_value`: `-0.0` replaces `0.0`), and wakes the ref's waiters either way. Effect v4.0.6 has no `TxRef.test.ts`; the 15 titles of the `Effect.test.ts` transaction block are in `tests/oracle/test_transaction.py`, and `tests/test_transaction.py` covers interruption (an interrupted retry unregisters its waiter, an interrupted body commits nothing), defects, conflicts under concurrency and the journal. Contention (`uv run python benchmarks/transaction.py`, best of 5, CPython 3.14 on an M-series Mac): 91k uncontended transactions/s (Effect under Node: 515k/s); 100 fibers each committing 100 increments to one `TxRef`, yielding between read and write, 1.8k commits/s with 49.5 reruns per commit, the same rerun count Effect's own code gives under Node for the same program (12.3k commits/s). About a sixth of an uncontended transaction is the context switch that provides `Transaction`.

**TxChunk, TxHashMap and TxHashSet (2026-10-27): done.** `fx.TxChunk[A]` (`pyfect.tx_chunk`, a `tuple` in a `TxRef`), `fx.TxHashMap[K, V]` (`pyfect.tx_hash_map`, a copy-on-write `dict`) and `fx.TxHashSet[V]` (`pyfect.tx_hash_set`, a `frozenset`), all invariant (Effect's `in out`), with every v4 function as a method: TxChunk `make` / `from_iterable` (any iterable), `empty` (`TxChunk[int].empty()`), `get`, `set`, `modify`, `update`, `append`, `prepend`, `append_all`, `prepend_all`, `concat`, `size`, `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `take`, `drop`, `slice`, `map`, `filter`; TxHashMap `make(*pairs)`, `from_iterable`, `empty`, `get` / `get_hash` / `modify` (an `Option`: `Some` or `None`, since a value may be `None`), `has`, `has_hash`, `set`, `remove`, `clear`, `size`, `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `modify_at`, `keys` / `values` / `entries` / `to_entries` / `to_values` (lists), `snapshot` (a read-only `Mapping`), `union` (any `Mapping`), `set_many`, `remove_many`, `map`, `filter` (a `TypeIs` predicate narrows), `filter_map` (`Ok` keeps), `reduce`, `has_by`, `some`, `every`, `find_first` (a pair or `None`), `for_each`, `flat_map`, `compact` (only on a map of `Option`s, a self-type); TxHashSet `make(*values)`, `from_iterable`, `from_hash_set` (any `AbstractSet`), `empty`, `add`, `remove`, `has`, `size`, `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `clear`, `union`, `intersection`, `difference`, `is_subset`, `some`, `every`, `map`, `filter`, `reduce`, `to_hash_set` (the `frozenset`). `TxX(ref)` wraps an existing `TxRef` (Effect's `makeUnsafe`; `.ref` reads it back). Each operation is one transactional step (`pyfect/_tx_ops.py`), so a read-then-write such as `remove`, `concat` or `union` is atomic even outside `fx.tx`; iterables passed to constructors, `set_many` and `remove_many` are read when called, so a one-shot iterator survives reruns, and constructors make a new ref on every run. As in Effect, `remove`, `modify` and `modify_at` of a missing key write nothing and wake no `tx_retry` waiter; as with Effect's HAMT, a map write that leaves every value the same (`Object.is`: setting a key to the value it has, `remove_many` of missing keys, an empty `set_many`) keeps the same `dict`, so it still wakes waiters but leaves the ref's `version` alone and reruns no concurrent reader (`_set_all` / `_without` in `tx_hash_map.py` are the only places the dict is rebuilt); read-only operations never write (Effect's `TxChunk.size` / `isEmpty` go through `modify`, a write of the same value that wakes waiters for nothing). Every title of `TxChunk.test.ts` (25), `TxHashMap.test.ts` (53) and `TxHashSet.test.ts` (32) is in `tests/oracle/test_tx_chunk.py`, `test_tx_hash_map.py`, `test_tx_hash_set.py` (`isTx*` guards are `isinstance`; `get_hash` finds nothing unless the hash is `hash(key)`. Since the core-gaps pass the maps hold Effect's HAMT `HashMap` / `HashSet` with Effect's `Hash.hash`, so `findFirst` returns Effect's entry and the expected values are Effect's own); `tests/test_tx_collections.py` pins `slice` / `take` / `drop` on negative and oversized bounds and which writes wake a waiter, both printed by Effect under Node, plus rollback, snapshots and `flat_map` failures. HAMT decision (`uv run python benchmarks/tx_collections.py`, best of 5, CPython 3.14 on an M-series Mac): one committed write of a new value (`modify`) costs 10.6 µs on a 10-entry map (a bare `TxRef.update` costs 10.6 µs), 14.4 µs at 1,000 entries, 33.6 µs at 10,000 and 258 µs at 100,000; `get` stays at 10.3 to 10.7 µs. Copying a 10,000-entry dict (about 23 µs) costs about twice the transaction itself, and a pure-Python HAMT would put a trie walk in every `get` and `has`, which stay at dict speed now, so no HAMT: revisit for maps of 100,000 entries or more under frequent writes, or batch several writes of one transaction into one copy first.

**TxQueue, TxDeferred, TxSemaphore, TxReentrantLock and TxPriorityQueue (2026-10-27): done.** `fx.TxQueue[A, E = Never]` (`pyfect.tx_queue` package; views `fx.TxEnqueue` / `fx.TxDequeue`, protocols contravariant / covariant as `Queue`'s, `clear` on the dequeue side since it gives items back): `bounded` / `unbounded` / `dropping` / `sliding` classmethods (`TxQueue[int, Done].bounded(8)`, capacity `int | None`, a negative capacity a `ValueError` when described), `offer`, `offer_all` (one transaction: a bounded queue waits until every item fits), `take`, `poll`, `peek`, `take_all`, `take_n`, `take_between`, `clear`, `size`, `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `is_full`, `fail`, `fail_cause`, `end` (self-typed `TxEnqueue[Any, Done]`, so `TxQueue[int, str | Done]` can end), `interrupt`, `shutdown`, `is_open` / `is_closing` / `is_done` / `is_shutdown`, `await_completion`, with Effect's Open → Closing → Done states (`fail` drops the items and is Done at once; `fail_cause` / `end` / `interrupt` drain first; a done queue's takers fail with its cause, so an ended one fails with `Done()`). `fx.TxDeferred[A, E = Never]` (`make`, `wait` for `await`, `poll`, `done(Result)`, `succeed`, `fail`); `fx.TxSemaphore` (`make` dies on a negative count, `capacity` a property, `available`, `acquire`, `acquire_n`, `try_acquire`, `try_acquire_n`, `release`, `release_n` capped at the capacity, counts of 0 or less die as in Effect, `with_permit(eff)`, `with_permits(eff, n)`, `with_permit_scoped`); `fx.TxReentrantLock` (per-fiber counts in a frozen `LockState`: `acquire_read` / `acquire_write` / `release_read` / `release_write` returning counts, `read_lock` / `write_lock` scoped (released for the acquiring fiber whoever closes the scope), `with_read_lock` / `with_write_lock` / `with_lock`, `read_locks`, `write_locks`, `locked`, `read_locked`, `write_locked`); `fx.TxPriorityQueue[A]` with Effect's `Order` as a `key=` function (`empty(key=)` spelled `TxPriorityQueue[int].empty()`, `from_iterable(items, key=)`, `make(*items, key=)`; without `key` the items must be comparable, which the overloads check), `size`, `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `peek`, `peek_option`, `offer`, `offer_all`, `take`, `take_all`, `take_option`, `take_up_to`, `remove_if`, `retain_if`, `to_list` (`toArray`); equal keys keep insertion order and `offer_all` merges by galloping then bisecting, as Effect's `mergeSorted` (Effect's comparison-count titles count `<` calls on the keys). Every operation is one transactional step (`_tx_ops.decide`: read and write the journal, return a success, a failure or `tx_retry`). One deliberate difference: Effect's `withPermit` / `withLock` / scoped `acquireRelease({ interruptible: true })` run the acquiring transaction inside `restore`, so an interrupt that lands after the commit and before the release is guarded keeps the permit or lock forever (a `MaxOpsBeforeYield` sweep in `tests/test_tx_coordination.py` shows the leak); here `fx.transaction.tx_restoring(restore, eff)` runs only the body and its `tx_retry` wait under `restore`, and the commit and the guard that follows stay uninterruptible. Every title of `TxQueue.test.ts` (91), `TxDeferred.test.ts` (18), `TxSemaphore.test.ts` (22), `TxReentrantLock.test.ts` (29) and `TxPriorityQueue.test.ts` (24) is in `tests/oracle/test_tx_queue.py`, `test_tx_deferred.py`, `test_tx_semaphore.py`, `test_tx_reentrant_lock.py`, `test_tx_priority_queue.py` (type guards are `isinstance`; data-last `pipe` titles call the method; the fractional / `NaN` counts of one TxQueue title are their normalized `int`s). Review: Effect's "writer blocks readers" / "reader blocks writer" titles fork the waiter without starting it, so they passed with either exclusion check deleted; `tests/test_tx_coordination.py` now starts the waiter and checks it stays out, and also pins `fail` dropping the items, `acquire_n` waiting for all `n` permits, equal-key order through `offer` / `offer_all` and `take_up_to(-1)` (both printed by Effect under Node), and one reentrant read lock released per closed scope. Releasing a lock the fiber does not hold now writes nothing, as in Effect (it had woken every `tx_retry` waiter on the lock). `TxDequeue.clear` now takes `Done` out of the error type as `TxQueue.clear` does (Effect's `ExcludeDone`).

**TxPubSub and TxSubscriptionRef (2026-10-27): done.** `fx.TxPubSub[A]` (`pyfect.tx_pubsub`, invariant): `bounded` / `unbounded` / `dropping` / `sliding` classmethods (`TxPubSub[int].bounded(16)`, a negative capacity a `ValueError` when described), `strategy`, `capacity` (`None` when unbounded, Effect's `Infinity`, as `TxQueue`), `size` (the fullest subscriber), `is_empty`, `is_non_empty`, `is_full`, `is_shutdown`, `publish`, `publish_all` (read once when called, so a one-shot iterator survives reruns), `subscribe` (scoped), `acquire_subscriber` / `release_subscriber`, `shutdown`, `await_shutdown`. Each subscriber gets a `TxQueue` of the hub's strategy and capacity, and a publish offers to all of them in one transaction: a bounded hub's publisher waits until every subscriber has room, so none receives a value early. Subscribers get the read side, `TxDequeue[A, Never]` (Effect hands out the whole `TxQueue`, so a subscriber could offer into its own queue); `acquire_subscriber` / `release_subscriber` join the running transaction or run their own rather than requiring `Transaction`, as every pyfect `Tx*` operation does, and releasing a queue the hub no longer holds does nothing. `fx.TxSubscriptionRef[A]` (`pyfect.tx_subscription_ref`): `make`, `ref`, `pubsub`, `get`, `modify`, `set`, `update`, `get_and_set`, `get_and_update`, `update_and_get`, `changes` (a scoped `TxDequeue` whose first value is the current one, offered in the subscribing transaction) and `changes_stream` (a `Stream` subscribing when it starts, `unwrap` of `from_effect_repeat(take)`). A write publishes in its transaction, so a rolled-back one publishes nothing. Every title of `TxPubSub.test.ts` (26) and `TxSubscriptionRef.test.ts` (17) is in `tests/oracle/test_tx_pubsub.py` and `test_tx_subscription_ref.py`; `tests/test_tx_pubsub.py` pins rollback, all-or-nothing bounded publishing, a dropped value in `publish_all`, shutdown interrupting a waiting taker, double release, and `changes_stream` following later writes. Review: Effect's titles use one subscriber, so `size` summing instead of taking the fullest, `is_full` requiring every subscriber full, and `changes` never releasing its subscriber all passed; `tests/test_tx_pubsub.py` now pins each (two subscribers holding 1 and 2 values; no subscriber left after a closed `changes` scope). Interrupting a blocked bounded publish delivers to no subscriber, a subscriber joining mid-wait gets the value, and a shut-down `TxSubscriptionRef` pubsub ends `changes_stream` with an interrupt, on the sync and asyncio runtimes. `benchmarks/runtime.py`: map chain 3.16M steps/s, gen 2.22M, fork+join 162k/s, as before.

**M9 completeness pass (2026-10-27): done; M9 is complete.** A script check of the exit-criteria files finds every title ported, one pytest per title with the title in its docstring and none omitted: the `Effect.test.ts` transaction block 15 of 15, `TxChunk.test.ts` 25, `TxHashMap.test.ts` 53, `TxHashSet.test.ts` 32, `TxQueue.test.ts` 91, `TxDeferred.test.ts` 18, `TxSemaphore.test.ts` 21, `TxReentrantLock.test.ts` 28, `TxPriorityQueue.test.ts` 24, `TxPubSub.test.ts` 26, `TxSubscriptionRef.test.ts` 17 (350 titles; Effect v4.0.6 has no `TxRef.test.ts`). Every export of the eleven `Tx*.ts` modules and `Effect.tx` / `txRetry` / `Transaction` exists, but for the `isTx*` guards (`isinstance`), `makeUnsafe` (the constructor) and `TxPriorityQueue.toArray` (`to_list`). The M8 Schema deferrals `ErrorInstance` / `Defect` JSON forms (§5 question 11), `TaggedStruct`, `TaggedUnion` / `toTaggedUnion` and `StructWithRest` are done too (`Opaque` maps to `S.Class`), and the Schema and Transactions guides are on the docs site. New guard: two tx-core mutants (an uninterruptible body, a retry flag never cleared) were caught only because the suite hung, so `tests/_timeout.py` fails any test still running after `test_timeout` seconds (60 by default, a `SIGALRM` timer, no new dependency; `tests/test_hang_timeout.py` checks it on a `tx_retry` nothing wakes). The full suite has 6,783 tests (one skipped). Performance (`benchmarks/runtime.py`, best of 5, run alternately with the M8 commit): map chain 3.13–3.22M vs 3.14–3.16M steps/s, generator steps 2.26–2.27M vs 2.18–2.21M, fork + join 165–169k vs 166–167k/s, within noise. `benchmarks/transaction.py`: about 90k uncontended tx/s (Effect under Node: 515k). Deferred: the typed JSON Schema of an `extra_items=` struct (permissive, `additionalProperties: true`), a per-case `TaggedUnion.match` (Python has no mapped types; the `match` statement is checked by pyright instead), and Schema's long tail (`TemplateLiteral`, `BigDecimal`, network schemas, `SchemaRepresentation`).

### M10+ — Platform (survey order; each wave depends on the core milestones it names)

**M10 wave 1 (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.platform_error`: `PlatformError(reason)` over `BadArgument` and a `SystemError` subclass per tag (`AlreadyExists`, `BadResource`, `Busy`, `InvalidData`, `NotFound`, `PermissionDenied`, `TimedOut`, `UnexpectedEof`, `Unknown`, `WouldBlock`, `WriteZero`), `system_error` / `bad_argument`, and `from_exception` tagging an `OSError` by `errno` as Effect's Node platform tags its codes. `pyfect.path`: the `Path` service (`posix`, `win32`, `layer_posix`, `layer_win32`, `layer` for the host) with every member of Effect's, `resolve_within` and strict `from_file_url` / `to_file_url`. `pyfect.file_system`: the `FileSystem` service and `File` handle with every operation of Effect's (`access` … `write_file`, `stream`, `sink`, `watch`), `LiveFileSystem` / `layer` on `os` (reads and writes on worker threads, its own cursor per `File` with `pread` / `pwrite`), `NoopFileSystem` / `layer_noop`, and `WatchBackend`. `pyfect.stdio` (`Stdio`, `layer`, `layer_test`) and `pyfect.terminal` (`Terminal`, `layer`, `live(should_quit=, input=, output=)`: line and key input from a buffer filled by short waits on a worker thread, raw mode on a TTY only while `read_input`'s scope is open, Node's readline key names). `platform.layer` merges the four (Effect's `NodeServices`). The M7 deferrals are done: `ConfigProvider.from_dot_env` / `from_dir` read through `FileSystem` and `Path`, and `Logger.to_file` is ported. `run_main` is final: a signal interrupts the main fiber with its own id, and after a signal the process exits with the teardown's status even when it is 0. Every title of `FileSystem.test-utils.ts` (44) and `NodeFileSystem.test.ts` but its `node:fs` precision mocks (7), `Path.test.ts` and `NodePath.test.ts` (5), `Stdio.test.ts` and `NodeStdio.test.ts` (4), and `NodeTerminal.test.ts` but its `Prompt` cases and readline disposal (7) are in `tests/oracle/test_file_system.py`, `test_path.py`, `test_stdio.py`, `test_terminal.py`; the fromDotEnv / fromDir ports now mock `FileSystem` with `NoopFileSystem` subclasses, as Effect's do with `layerNoop`. `tests/test_platform.py` pins the pyfect-specific behaviour (errno tags, `os.path` semantics, BOMs, naive datetimes, unsigned device numbers, `Logger.to_file` batches, the live standard streams); `tests/test_run_main.py` pins the two signal changes. `StdioService` and `TerminalService` reject non-`Effect` fields when built (the public-API guard in `tests/test_effect_arguments.py` found `layer_test` unguarded). The full suite has 6,906 tests (one skipped). The review found and fixed, against Node 24 (pinned in `tests/test_platform.py`): `copy` was a hand-rolled tree walk, so a directory copied into its own subdirectory recursed until the path was too long, a copy onto itself or a file onto a directory silently did nothing, missing destination parents failed and symbolic links were dereferenced; it now follows Node's `fs.cp` (`file_system/_copy.py`: the same refusals, tagged `Unknown` as Effect tags Node's `ERR_FS_CP_*`, parents made, links copied with absolute targets). `glob` kept files under an excluded directory and a trailing separator; `from_file_url` rejected `file://localhost/...` and kept `.` / `..` segments, which Effect's parsed `URL` never has; `read_file_string` died on a non-text codec (`rot13`, `base64`) instead of a `BadArgument`; concurrent terminal reads could append their bytes out of order (one descriptor reader at a time now); and `Logger.to_file` wrote a lone surrogate as `?` instead of `TextEncoder`'s U+FFFD (`_js.text_encode` is now the one `TextEncoder`, shared with `write_file_string` and the seeded Random).

1. PlatformError, Path, FileSystem (M6), Stdio / Terminal, final `run_main`
2. ChildProcess; encoding framers (Ndjson / SSE / JsonArray via Channel: done in M6, schema variants in M8)
3. HttpClient on httpx; socket; net
4. sql core + asyncpg / psycopg / aiosqlite drivers (M8, M4 Pool and Request)
5. cli
6. ASGI http server / router → http-api (M8 JSON Schema) → rpc
7. persistence (RateLimiter / CircuitBreaker can come early), workers
8. `pyfect[otel]` bridge, Prometheus formatter (both done in M5)
9. workflow → ai (wrapping SDKs, MCP) → graphql, reactivity, devtools, eventlog, cluster
10. M10g: the cluster runtime (Entity → Sharding, Runners, RunnerServer → ClusterCron, EntityProxy, ClusterWorkflowEngine, socket and HTTP runners), then the Atom family of `effect/reactivity`

**Exit criteria:** port each package's test suite as it lands.

**M10 wave 2, ChildProcess (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.process`: `make(command, args, *, cwd, env, extend_env, shell, detached, windows_hide, stdin, stdout, stderr, additional_fds, kill_signal, force_kill_after)` builds a `StandardCommand`, `pipe_to(that, from_=, to=)` a `PipedCommand`, with `prefix`, `set_cwd`, `set_env`, `flatten_command`, `parse_fd_name` / `fd_name`; a command is an `Effect[ChildProcessHandle, PlatformError, ChildProcessSpawner | Scope]`. `ChildProcessHandle` (`pid`, `exit_code`, `is_running`, `kill`, `stdin` sink, `stdout` / `stderr` / `all` streams, `get_input_fd` / `get_output_fd`, `unref`) and `ChildProcessSpawnerService(spawn)` with `exit_code`, `string`, `lines`, `stream_string`, `stream_lines`. `process.layer` (on `FileSystem` and `Path`) follows `NodeChildProcessSpawner`: process groups for detached children, `kill_signal` then `SIGKILL` after `force_kill_after`, the group drained on scope release, pipelines killed in reverse, `unref` per process; `platform.layer` now provides it. Every title of `ChildProcess.test.ts` but one (29), of the `ChildProcessSpawnerTest` suite (75 tests) and of `NodeChildProcessSpawner.test.ts` but its two Windows `taskkill` mocks are in `tests/oracle/test_child_process.py` and `test_child_process_spawner.py` (fixtures in `tests/oracle/fixtures/process/`, Effect's bash scripts and Python versions of its TypeScript ones); `tests/test_process.py` pins the pyfect-specific behaviour (asyncio and `run_sync`, interruption kills the child, no stall while waiting, boundary validation, `shell`, `env` removal, more concurrent pipelines and force kills than a thread pool has workers, a failed input stream winning over an earlier exit, and, checked under Node, an unref-ed child's output readable after its scope closes). The full suite has 7,049 tests (one skipped).

**M10 wave 3, HttpClient on httpx (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.http` (`fx.http`): `method` (`HttpMethod` with `QUERY`), `headers` (an immutable lowercased `Headers` mapping, `CurrentRedactedNames`, HTTP dates and `Retry-After` parsed to `Seconds` or UTC `datetime`), `url_params` (`UrlParams`, `URLSearchParams` encoding, `redact_url`), `url` (a WHATWG-style parser over `SplitResult`, checked against Node's `URL`, and the `set_*` updates), `cookies` (`Cookies`, RFC 6265 validation, `Set-Cookie` parsing and serializing), `body` (`Empty`, `Raw`, `Bytes`, `FormData`, `Stream`, `json` / `json_schema`, `file` / `file_from_info` with exact ranges), `request` (a frozen `HttpClientRequest` whose operations are methods, `compress_body` gzip / deflate), `response` (`HttpClientResponse` with memoized `bytes_` / `text` / `json`, `match_status`, `filter_status_ok`, `schema_json`, `schema_no_body`, `schema_body_json` with a reviver), `error` (`HttpClientError` over the six reasons, URLs redacted in messages and `to_json`), `trace_context` (W3C and B3) and `client`: `HttpClientService` with Effect's combinators as methods (`filter_status_ok`, `retry_transient` honouring `Retry-After`, `follow_redirects` dropping credentials across origins, `with_cookies_ref`, `with_scope`, `catch_all`, ...), `catch_tag` / `transform_response` for `.pipe`, a client span per request with the semantic attributes and propagation, `make(send)` for backends, `layer_test(handler)`. `pyfect.http.httpx_client` (extra `pyfect[httpx]`, never imported by `pyfect`): `layer` / `layer_with(**httpx_options)` / `make` on an `httpx.AsyncClient` driven on its own asyncio loop thread, so it works under `run_sync` and `run_async`; bodies stream both ways and interruption (or an unread response collected by `weakref.finalize`) closes the request. Every title of `http/Headers.test.ts`, `Cookies.test.ts`, `UrlParams.test.ts`, `Url.test.ts`, `HttpBody.test.ts`, `HttpTraceContext.test.ts`, `HttpClientRequest.test.ts` and `http/HttpClient.test.ts` but the schema, web-conversion and JavaScript-registry cases (its `withRateLimiter` and `withCircuitBreaker` cases were ported in the M10d completeness pass), and the applicable titles of `HttpClient.test.ts`, `FetchHttpClient.test.ts` and `NodeHttpClient.test.ts` against a local stdlib server, are in `tests/oracle/test_headers.py`, `test_cookies.py`, `test_url_params.py`, `test_url.py`, `test_http_body.py`, `test_http_trace_context.py`, `test_http_client_request.py`, `test_http_client.py`, `test_http_client_server.py`, `test_fetch_http_client.py` and `test_node_http_client.py` (187 tests; the server ones skip without httpx). `tests/test_http_client.py` pins `layer_test`, aborting unread responses and httpx closing the connection on interruption. The review found and fixed: a `Redacted` URL parameter whose name held a lone surrogate showed its secret in the span's `url.full` and `url.query` (redacted names are now compared as `toWellFormed` gives them, `_js.to_well_formed`); an unread httpx response collected after its layer closed raised from its finalizer (`_Backend._call_soon` is the one way onto the loop and does nothing once it has closed); `file_from_info` accepted lengths past `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`; and `catch_all` was a curried function whose callback parameter pyright could not type, so it is a method (it removes the whole error type, which a method binds safely). The skeptical review then fixed: `url.parse` read `file:///etc/passwd` as host `etc`, kept `127.1` and `0x7f.0.0.1` as domains, wrote IPv4-mapped IPv6 hosts dotted and kept `file://localhost` (it now follows WHATWG's file-host, IPv4 and IPv6 serializers, and `tests/test_http_url.py` pins 78 `href`s printed by Node's `URL`; an empty but present `?` or `#` still disappears, as a `SplitResult` cannot hold it); and `with_scope` tied every request made under it, including another client's inside a `tap`, to its scope, where Effect ties only the request it sends (the scoped controller now carries that request and `_send` checks identity, checked against Effect under Node). `tests/test_http_client.py` now also pins that interrupting a request or a body read aborts the backend's signal (removing either abort failed no test before). The full suite has 7,323 tests (one skipped).

**M10 wave 3, socket and net (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.net`: `net_address` (IP addresses as `ipaddress` values, MAC, Inet and Unix socket addresses), `host` (domain names, host/port parsing), `ip_network`, `ip_interface`, `dns` (validated record dataclasses, `DnsError`, `make`, `layer_static`, and `make_system` / `layer_system` on `getaddrinfo` / `gethostbyaddr`, each call on a thread of its own; the standard library has no record queries, so the system `resolve` fails with `Unsupported`) and `address_resolver`; all parsing happens at the boundary. `pyfect.socket`: the `Socket` model (`Reader`, `Writer`, `SocketService`, `CloseEvent`, `SocketError` over its five reasons), `reader_bytes`, `reader_string` (a `TextDecoder`: `_js.text_decoder`, shared with `Channel.decode_text`), `to_channel`, `to_channel_string`, `to_stream`, `make_channel`; live TCP, Unix and TLS sockets (`make_net`, `make_unix`, `make_tls`, `from_socket` for Effect's `fromDuplex`, channels, layers) on a shared daemon asyncio loop thread, so they work under `run_sync` and `run_async`; TLS on our own `SSLObject` session, which allows Node's half-close and upgrades on either side; `from_web_socket` over any `WebSocketLike` (the browser's `ready_state`, listeners, `send`, `close`, optional `pause` / `resume`), `make_web_socket` / `make_web_socket_channel` / `layer_web_socket` through `WebSocketConstructor`. `pyfect.socket.server`: `SocketServer`, `SocketServerError`, `make`, `make_unix`, `make_tls`, `layer`, `layer_tls`. `pyfect.socket.ws` (extra `pyfect[websockets]`, never imported by `pyfect`): `WsWebSocket`, `layer_web_socket_constructor`, `layer_web_socket`, and `make_server` / `layer_server` (Effect's `NodeSocketServer.makeWebSocket`, handlers get the native socket as `WebSocket`). Every title of `Socket.test.ts`, the net tests but their schema and JavaScript-only cases, `NodeSocket.test.ts` but its `vi.mock` spies and the `TransformStream` group, and the hosts-file case of `Dns.test-utils.ts` are in `tests/oracle/test_socket.py`, `test_node_socket.py`, `test_node_socket_web_socket.py`, `test_net_address.py`, `test_host.py`, `test_ip_network.py`, `test_ip_interface.py`, `test_dns.py`, `test_address_resolver.py`; `tests/test_socket_live.py` and `tests/test_web_socket.py` pin the rest (half-open connections, late connections after server close, races with the loop thread). The socket test modules turn `ResourceWarning` into errors, so a leaked transport fails its test. Not ported: `fromTransformStream` (web streams), the net schemas (`DatagramSocket` came in the M10a completeness pass). The review found and fixed: `reader_string` kept a UTF-8 byte-order mark that `TextDecoder` drops; text with a lone surrogate raised from a write instead of being sent as `TextEncoder` encodes it (one `encode_chunk` on `_js.text_encode` now); a write made while a TLS upgrade's handshake ran went out in plaintext and broke the handshake (the upgrade now starts in the order of the fiber's loop calls and the session queues plaintext until it is done, as Node's `TLSSocket` does); and the WebSocket client, which v4's `Socket` has, was missing.

**M10a completeness pass (2026-10-28): done; M10a (waves 1 to 3) is complete.** A title-by-title script check of every Effect test file for the M10a modules (the platform tests in `effect/test`, `effect/test/http`, `socket`, `net`, `process`, and `platform-node(-shared)/test` for FileSystem, Path, Stdio, Terminal, ChildProcessSpawner, Socket, DatagramSocket, HttpClient, AddressResolver and Dns) finds every title ported or omitted with a reason in its oracle module's docstring. It found one gap: `DatagramSocket`, which v4's `effect/socket` has and the socket cluster had left out. It is now `pyfect.socket.datagram`: the model (`DatagramSocket` tag, `DatagramSocketService`, `Reader` with `pull` / `address` / `dropped()` / `join_multicast`, `Writer` with `write` / `write_all`, `Datagram` and `ReceivedDatagram` whose sender address is parsed on first read, `OutgoingDatagram`, `ReceiveBuffer` with `"dropping"` / `"sliding"`, `DatagramSocketError` over its five reasons), `make_from_backing_socket` (one reader at a time; an interrupted open is left to finish and its socket closed before the next reader binds; sticky read errors after the queued packets; writes wait for a reader) and the live adapter `make_udp` / `layer_udp` / `from_socket` on Python's `socket` module (Effect's `NodeDatagramSocket`: names resolved once per reader, `Peer` or `Connect`, `reuse_address`, buffer sizes, TTLs, multicast egress, ICMP reports to `on_error`, errno-mapped error kinds). Every title of `DatagramSocket.test.ts` (12) and `NodeDatagramSocket.test.ts` (4) is in `tests/oracle/test_datagram_socket.py` and `test_node_datagram_socket.py`; `tests/test_datagram_socket.py` pins 12 more (IPv6, a connected socket's refused packets, address in use, a failed lookup, multicast on the loopback interface, reader exclusivity and writes waiting for a reader on real sockets), and `tests/type_checks.py::check_datagram_socket` pins the types and that an IPv4 group cannot take an interface index, a remote cannot be both `Peer` and `Connect`, and `make_from_backing_socket` removes `Scope` only. Whole files left for later waves, with the reason: `HttpDecompression.test.ts`, `HttpCompression.test.ts`, `Template.test.ts`, `Mime.test.ts` and the `FindMyWay` suites (HTTP server, wave 6); the rate-limiter and circuit-breaker titles of `http/HttpClient.test.ts` (persistence, wave 7); `NodeStream.test.ts` and `NodeSink.test.ts` (Node `Readable` / `Writable` interop, decisions table); `cli/TerminalCapabilities.test.ts` (cli, wave 5); the CoreDNS-container cases of `Dns.test-utils.ts` (the standard library has no record queries); `encoding/Base64Url.test.ts` (the Base64 / Hex encoding module is not ported; the schema codecs cover the same alphabet). Known difference: a packet wakes a parked `pull` through the scheduler, so it completes on the next scheduler turn, not inside the platform's callback ("resumes an already-parked pull before the native packet callback returns" is adapted). Performance (`benchmarks/runtime.py`, three runs): map chain 3.29–3.32M steps/s, generator steps 2.35–2.37M, fork + join 174–175k/s, against 3.13–3.22M, 2.26–2.27M and 165–169k/s recorded at M9 and 3.14M / 2.29M / 172k in the socket review, so no regression. The full suite has 7,551 tests (one skipped).

**M10 wave 4, sql core (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.sql`: `error` (`SqlError(reason)` over Effect's eleven reasons with `is_retryable`, `ResultLengthMismatch`, `is_sql_error`, `is_sql_error_reason`, `classify_sqlite_error` reading `sqlite3`'s `sqlite_errorname` / `sqlite_errorcode` or a code mapping), `connection` (the `Connection` protocol, `Acquirer`, `Borrower`), `statement` (`Fragment` and its segments, `Statement[A]` as an `Effect` with `raw`, `values`, `values_unprepared`, `unprepared`, `without_transform`, `stream`, `compile`; the `Constructor` with `unsafe`, `literal`, `identifier`, `in_`, `insert`, `update`, `update_values`, `and_`, `or_`, `csv`, `join`, `on_dialect`; `make_compiler`, `make_compiler_sqlite`, `default_transforms`, `SpanPropagationEnabled`, the connection wait-time span attribute), `client` (`SqlClientService` with `with_transaction` (spans, savepoints, the per-transaction semaphore, `BeginTransaction`, `release_savepoint` opt-in, failed-commit cleanup), `after_commit`, `reserve`, `reactive`, `reactive_mailbox`; `make`, `make_with_transaction`, `transaction_connection`, `make_sqlite_acquirers` with connection poisoning), `resolver` (`ordered`, `grouped`, `find_by_id`, `void`, `request`, batching by transaction), `schema` (`find_all`, `find_non_empty`, `find_one`, `find_one_option`, `void`), `stream` (`async_pause_resume`) and `migrator` (`make`, `from_record`, `from_file_system`, `MigrationError`). `pyfect.reactivity` (needed by the client): `ReactivityService`, `Reactivity`, `layer`, `mutation`, `query`, `stream`, `invalidate`, `with_batch`. `pyfect.sql.sqlite`, the reference driver on the standard library's `sqlite3` (no extra): `SqliteClientConfig`, `SqliteClientService` (`config`, `backup`, `load_extension`), `SqliteClient`, `make`, `layer`, `layer_config` (a `Config` of the whole config or one per field, Effect's `Config.Wrap`), and `sqlite.migrator` (`run`, `layer`); every call runs on the connection's own worker thread and resumes the fiber through the scheduler, so no fiber blocks; WAL is enabled with retries until the busy timeout. Every title of `effect/test/sql` (`Statement`, `SqlError`, `SqlClient`, `SqlResolver`, `SqlSchema`) and of `sql-sqlite-node/test` `Client`, `Resolver` and `SqliteMigrator` is in `tests/oracle/test_sql_statement.py`, `test_sql_error.py`, `test_sql_client.py`, `test_sql_resolver.py`, `test_sql_schema.py`, `test_sqlite_client.py`, `test_sqlite_resolver.py`, `test_sqlite_migrator.py` (108 tests) but one omitted with its reason; `tests/test_sql_template.py`, `test_reactivity.py` and `test_sql_sqlite_worker.py` pin the template parser, Reactivity and the worker (21 tests), and `tests/type_checks.py::sql_statements` pins the types and that a runtime `str` cannot be SQL text. Not ported then: `SqlModel` and `SqlModelServices.test.ts` (they needed `Model` / `VariantSchema`; both came in M10f), the sqlite `KeyValueStore`, `Persistence` and `EventJournal` tests (those modules are not ported), `Migrator.fromGlob` / `fromBabelGlob` (bundler imports). The review found and fixed: a `sqlite3` error raised outside a statement (as `load_extension` raises) has no `sqlite_errorname`, and classifying it raised in the worker's done-callback, so the fiber was never resumed and the test stalled; the worker now computes the outcome on its own thread with every exception (including one from the classifier) turned into a failure or a defect, so the callback only resumes (`tests/test_sql_sqlite_worker.py`). The public-API guard found the new effect-taking methods unguarded (`with_transaction`, `after_commit`, `reactive`, the Reactivity functions); they call `require_effect`. The skeptical review found and fixed: (1) the exported `Literal` segment took any `str`, so `sql("SELECT {}", Literal(user_input))` put runtime text into SQL with pyright's approval; its text is now a `LiteralString` and `literal_unsafe` is the explicit way around it (pinned in `tests/type_checks.py::sql_statements`, which also type-checks under `--pythonversion 3.14` for the t-string branch). (2) `Semaphore` woke every waiter on each release, and each woken fiber that found the permit taken parked again, so N fibers contending cost O(N²); the SQLite client sends every statement through one, so 5,000 concurrent statements ran at 37/s against 29k/s one after another. Effect resumes a waiter at once, so its first waiter takes the permit and the loop stops (31–35k/s at 1,000 and 4,000 fibers under Node). A release now wakes only the waiters its free permits can serve, counting the permits promised to woken waiters until they retry; a waiter interrupted after its wake-up hands it on (`_Turn` in `semaphore.py`), and `take` stays interruptible up to its retry as Effect's does. Contended `with_permits` went from 350/s to 75k/s at 4,000 fibers and concurrent SQLite statements from 37/s to 25k/s (`tests/test_primitives.py` pins the hand-off, the order of a waiter too large for a release, and the scaling). (3) `layerConfig` was missing. Mutation testing found untested logic, now covered: the empty-`IN` line-comment check, a nested fragment's compile overwriting its own cache entry, `update`'s `omit` (`tests/test_sql_compiler.py`); the per-transaction semaphore serializing concurrent savepoints, `make_sqlite_acquirers` without `is_transaction` (no-transaction rollback counts as clean, poisoning clears after a successful rollback, the lease wait is interruptible) (`tests/test_sql_transactions.py`); and a query invalidated while running runs once more (`tests/test_reactivity.py`). Interrupting a fiber whose statement is running on the worker thread waits for that statement before the rollback runs (Effect's `node:sqlite` cannot interrupt a statement at all). The full suite has 7,707 tests.

**M10 wave 4, PostgreSQL driver on psycopg (2026-10-28): done.** `pyfect.sql.pg` (extra `pyfect[pg]` on psycopg 3, never imported by `pyfect`): `config` (`PgConnectionConfig` → `PgClientConfig` → `PgPoolConfig`, frozen and checked when built: the URL and explicit fields, socket `path`, `ssl` as `True` or libpq TLS parameters, `password` as `Redacted` or an effect run per connection, `connect_timeout`, `keep_alive`, `startup_parameters` / `startup_options`, `types` as a psycopg `AdaptersMap`, `prepare`, `prepared_statement_cache_size`, the transforms, `on_acquire`, pool sizes, `idle_timeout`, `connection_ttl`, `acquire_timeout`), `connection` (`PgConnection`: `query`, `query_values`, `stream`, `listen` returning a `Dequeue` as v4 does, `pin`, `interrupt`, `process_id`; `make`), `pool` (`PgPool` on `Pool.make_with_ttl`: `get`, `reserve`, `use`, `invalidate`, sessions retired when they die or a cancel was not confirmed), `client` (`PgClient` with `json`, `listen`, `notify`; `make` (pooled), `make_client` (one session, optional `acquire_for_stream`), `layer`, `layer_config`, `layer_from`, `make_compiler`), `error` (Effect's SQLSTATE table, `classify` for psycopg errors) and `migrator` (`run`, `layer`, schema dumps through `pg_dump` with the client's libpq parameters and `PGPASSWORD` only). psycopg's asyncio API runs on one loop thread of its own (`_loop.py`); a fiber waits for a call through `Latch.wait`, so none blocks and the client works under `run_sync` and `run_async`. Interrupting a statement sends PostgreSQL a cancel and waits for the statement to end; a cancel not confirmed by `57014` (or an `interrupt` no statement confirmed) retires the session from the pool. Effect has no COPY ("COPY is not supported"), so neither does pyfect. Every applicable title of `sql-pg/test` `Client.integration`, `PgConnection` (config), `PgConnection.integration`, `PgPool.integration`, `PgPool.in-process` (failed startup, acquireTimeout), `PgConnection.in-process` (what the config resolves to: startup defaults, URL host and port, explicit fields over the URL, a socket directory as `host`, a password effect run per connection, `connect_timeout=0`), `PgMigrator`, `PgMigrator.integration`, `StartupParameters.integration`, `TransactionAcquire` and `SqlErrorClassification` is in `tests/oracle/test_pg_client.py`, `test_pg_connection.py`, `test_pg_connection_config.py`, `test_pg_pool.py`, `test_pg_migrator.py`, `test_pg_sql_error_classification.py` (124 tests), each omission with its reason in the module docstring; they run against a throwaway server that `tests/oracle/_postgres.py` starts with `initdb` / `pg_ctl` in a temporary directory (skipped without the binaries), with a TCP proxy that holds back cancel requests standing in for Effect's late-cancel `stream` factory.
